## S-DU YING DJÜN. 使徒行傳

### Sd. I.

<u><ruby><rb>提阿非羅</rb><rt>DI-O-FI-LO</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>教訓個</rb><rt>kao-hyüing-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>揀選個</rb><rt>kæn-shün-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>*tʽin*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>確實個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-jih-go</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>聚攏東</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等待</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸多少</rb><rt>m-to-siao</rt><rb>日數</rb><rt>nyih-su</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚攏東</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>時節</rb><rt>z-tsin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>Nyih-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>講好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看東</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>升上去</rb><rt>sing-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>看弗見</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看東</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>看東</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一間</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>樓頂</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多馬</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴多羅買</rb><rt>Pô-to-lo-mæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬太</rb><rt>Mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>亞勒腓</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-leh-fi-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>銳羅底</rb><rt>Ze-lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬利亞</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一心一意</rb><rt>ih-sing-ih-i</rt><rb>弗歇個</rb><rt>feh-hyih-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>聚攏東</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt><rb>門徒個</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>名數</rb><rt>ming-su</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>廿個</rb><rt>nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>當中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u>。）<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>應驗個</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>隊裏</rb><rt>de-li</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>非義之財</rb><rt>fi-yi-ts-dzæ</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>磕倒</rb><rt>kʽeh-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>豁開</rb><rt>hweh-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肚腸</rb><rt>du-dziang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> （<ruby><rb>通</rb><rt>Tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇丘</rb><rt>keh-kyʽiu</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>土名</rb><rt>tʽu-ming</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞革大馬</rb><rt>Üô-keh-da-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>血田</rb><rt>Hyüih-din</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>聖詩裏</rb><rt>Sing-s-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>空地</rb><rt>kʽong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>21, 22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>來來去去個</rb><rt>læ-læ-kyʽi-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>聚攏東個</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>揀</rb><rt>*kæn*</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再活個</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>舉薦</rb><rt>kyü-tsin</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>若瑟</rb><rt>Ziah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴撒巴</rb><rt>Pô-sah-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶士都</rb><rt>Yiu-z-tu</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬提亞</rb><rt>Mô-di-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>揀着個</rb><rt>kæn-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>辦事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒個</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末頭</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撮着</rb><rt>tsʽeh-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬提亞</rb><rt>Mô-di-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十一個</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>算進</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在內</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。

### Sd. II.

<ruby><rb>五旬節個</rb><rt>WU-SHING-TSIH-GO</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>Hweh-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹過</rb><rt>cʽü-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐的個</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>咸間</rb><rt>ʽen-kæn</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岔打岔</rb><rt>tsʽô-tang-tsʽô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講起</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>別處</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>*Ka*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>國打國</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚集攏</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懍懍動</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>Tu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt><rb>稀奇了</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講來講去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>根生土養</rb><rt>keng-sang-tʽu-yiang</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕提亞</rb><rt>Pʽô-di-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>米太</rb><rt>Mi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>以攔</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-læn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米所波大米</rb><rt>Mi-sô-po-da-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>加帕多家</rb><rt>Kyüô-pʽô-to-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>本都</rb><rt>Peng-tu</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>弗呂家</rb><rt>Feh-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>旁非利亞</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近個</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂彼亞</rb><rt>Li-pe-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩底</rb><rt>Keh-li-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拉伯</rb><rt>Üô-lah-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>*kôh-cʽü*</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>疑惑不決</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>講究</rb><rt>kông-kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>新酒</rb><rt>sing-tsiu</rt><rb>喫醉的了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Keh-go fong-sing ih yiang-kʽæ. 或話，箇個風聲一揚開。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, sing-li m-deo-jü. 或話，心裏嘸頭緒。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>十一個</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇樣</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>猜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>喫酒醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsiu-tse</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>酒醉</rb><rt>tsiu-tse</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約珥</rb><rt>Iah-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> 『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>結煞個</rb><rt>Kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血氣</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>老成人</rb><rt>lao-dzing-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>夢</rb><rt>mong</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>威嚴</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>光明個</rb><rt>kwông-ming-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>之前</rb><rt>ts-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>血紅</rb><rt>hyüih-*ʽong*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。』 

<sup>22</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>憑據</rb><rt>*bing-kyü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。）<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>惡手</rb><rt>ôh-siu</rt><rb>馱來</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>釘煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>*jih-z-kô-zông*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>解掉</rb><rt>ka-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>縛住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>眼前</rb><rt>ngæn-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>移弗動</rb><rt>yi-feh-dong</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>高興</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>暫寓</rb><rt>dzæn-nyü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>墳墓裏</rb><rt>*veng-mo-li*</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>陰司裏</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>爛糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>活命個</rb><rt>Weh-ming-go</rt><rb>路頭</rb><rt>lu-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>點</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>依心滿足</rb><rt>i-sing-mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。』 

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讓</rb><rt>nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>葬好</rb><rt>tsông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罰咒過了</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-ko-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>看來</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再活個</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>錯落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>陰司裏</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>爛糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ngô kʽo-yi. 或話，我可以。（原註）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*Næn-kæn*</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>賜落來</rb><rt>s-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏腳凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>話起來</rb><rt>wô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>確確實實</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh-jih-jih</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>釘煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戳心</rb><rt>cʽoh-sing</rt><rb>難過</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赦罪個</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>遠處個</rb><rt>yün-cʽü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>召攏來個</rb><rt>dziao-long-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>*kyʽün-kyʽün*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>橫綳勛流</rb><rt>w̆ang-pang-hyüing-liu</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>加湊個</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三千</rb><rt>sæn-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>牢牢</rb><rt>lao-lao</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>使徒個</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相通</rb><rt>siang-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼</rb><rt>pʽah</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕起來</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚帶攏</rb><rt>jü-tæn-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>打通</rb><rt>tang-tʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>Me-nyih</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤖼</rb><rt>pʽah</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>得救個</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。

### Sd. III.

<ruby><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽÔ-PUN-NYIH</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>孃胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拐腳個</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>揹起</rb><rt>pe-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>美</u>門</rb><rt>Me-meng</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>金子</rb><rt>Kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>𠡒</rb><rt>te</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>腳底</rb><rt>kyiah-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>腳㬹</rb><rt>kyiah-tsang</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>健爭了</rb><rt>gyin-tsang-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>跳起來</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立立</rb><rt>lih-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跳跳</rb><rt>tʽiao-tʽiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>美</u>門口</rb><rt>Me-meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>討飯</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>十分</rb><rt>jih-feng</rt><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>醫好個</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>拐腳</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>*siu*</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>奔攏</rb><rt>peng-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>所羅門</u>個</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng-go</rt><rb>廊屋</rb><rt>lông-oh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好像</rb><rt>hao-ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>解上去個</rb><rt>ka-zông-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>當面前</rb><rt>tông-min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>棄掉個</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>饒放</rb><rt>nyiao-fông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兇手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>根源</rb><rt>Keng-nyün</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>健爭起來</rb><rt>gyin-tsang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Kyüing-wông. 或話，君王。（原註）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>應了</rb><rt>ing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>棄邪歸正</rb><rt>kyʽi-zia-kwe-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>塗掉</rb><rt>du-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>安慰個</rb><rt>en-w̆e-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話個</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>列位</rb><rt>lih-we</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒母耳</rb><rt>Sah-meo-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直落來</rb><rt>ih-dzih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>後裔</rb><rt>ʽeo-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>設立個</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>天下</rb><rt>Tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Sd. IV.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>聖殿裏個</rb><rt>sing-din-li-go</rt><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>死仔再活個</rb><rt>si-ts-tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>難熬</rb><rt>næn-ngao</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>班房裏</rb><rt>pæn-vông-li</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>夜了</rb><rt>yia-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男個</rb><rt>nen-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該亞法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>族種</rb><rt>dzoh-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>當中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盤問</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>得着個</rb><rt>teh-djôh-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>盤問</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>*kwun-fu*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>起屋個</rb><rt>kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>棄掉個</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落頭</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>頭一塊</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>賜落來</rb><rt>s-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>侃侃而談</rb><rt>kʽæn-kʽæn-r-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識字個</rb><rt>sih-z-go</rt><rb>粗人</rb><rt>tsʽu-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇了</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>隊夥</rb><rt>de-ho</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>醫好個</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>大堂</rb><rt>do-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦法</rb><rt>bæn-fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉東</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>*keh-kyʽi*</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>*z-tʽi*</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>揚開</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>重重</rb><rt>djong-djong</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>警戒</rb><rt>kying-kyiæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>出管</rb><rt>cʽih-kwun</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>聽</rb><rt>Tʽing</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>過於</rb><rt>ko-ü</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>忖忖看</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng-kʽen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗得弗</rb><rt>feh-teh-feh</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>做過個</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>*kwun-fu*</rt><rb>打算弗出</rb><rt>tang-sön-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>醫好個</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>年紀</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十多</rb><rt>s-jih-to</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>*s-du*</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>隊夥</rb><rt>de-ho</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>作吵</rb><rt>tsôh-tsʽao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>癡心妄想</rb><rt>cʽü-sing-vông-siang</rt></ruby>？<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚集攏</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。」 <sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本丟</rb><rt>Peng-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傅油個</rb><rt>fu-yiu-go</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>定規個</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>29, 30</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>伸出</rb><rt>sing-cʽih</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做出來</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>膽量</rb><rt>tæn-liang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Kyi-toh *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* fu-yiu Cü-kwu. <u>基督</u>個解說，就是傅油主顧。（原註）</sup>

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>禱告好</rb><rt>Tao-kao-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚攏個</rb><rt>jü-long-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動起來</rb><rt>dong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>CONG</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>財物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通來通去</rb><rt>tʽong-læ-tʽong-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>缺少長短</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao-dziang-tön</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>居</rb><rt>ke</rt><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>賣掉仔</rb><rt>ma-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賣掉個</rb><rt>ma-diao-go</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby> <sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒個</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>*ming-z*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約西</rb><rt>Iah-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>勸化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。）<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一丘</rb><rt>ih-kyʽiu</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>賣掉仔</rb><rt>ma-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒個</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。
