### Sd. V.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒非喇</rb><rt>Sah-fi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>留落星</rb><rt>liu-lôh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相通個</rb><rt>siang-tʽong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>幾分</rb><rt>kyi-feng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>引動</rb><rt>ying-dong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>說謊</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>哄騙</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>留落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>田價</rb><rt>din-kô</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>田</rb><rt>*Din*</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>賣掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>*kô-din*</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>章程</rb><rt>công-dzing</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>念頭</rb><rt>nyiæn-deo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>說謊</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>對着</rb><rt>te-djôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>對着</rb><rt>te-djôh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕了</rb><rt>pʽô-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成殮</rb><rt>dzing-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抬出</rb><rt>dæ-cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>一個半</rb><rt>ih-go-pun</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>箇去</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇丘</rb><rt>Keh-kyʽiu</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt><rb>賣個</rb><rt>ma-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>Tsông</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門口頭</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>*nyü-nying*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>跌到</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斷了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>合教會</rb><rt>ʽEh-kyiao-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>怕了</rb><rt>pʽô-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>*siang-sing-go*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>*cü-kwu*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>所羅門</u>個</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng-go</rt><rb>廊屋裏</rb><rt>lông-oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>集隊</rb><rt>jih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男男女女</rb><rt>nen-nen-nyü-nyü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt></ruby>。）<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>Zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>大街</rb><rt>do-ka</rt><rb>市面</rb><rt>z-min</rt><rb>擋出</rb><rt>tông-cʽih</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>春凳</rb><rt>cʽing-teng</rt><rb>鋪板裏</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>影</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>影着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>團圈</rb><rt>Dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>邪鬼</rb><rt>zia-kyü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>抬勒來</rb><rt>dæ-leh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>醫好了</rb><rt>i-hao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>TSI-S-DEO</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>湊隊個</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>牢監個</rb><rt>lao-kæn-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>句打句</rb><rt>kyü-tang-kyü</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起早</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ô-wông. 或話，𢛟惶。（原註）</sup>

<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>傳攏</rb><rt>djün-long</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>差人</rb><rt>Tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗着</rb><rt>feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走轉來</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>牢監</rb><rt>Lao-kæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>牢牢靠靠</rb><rt>lao-lao-kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>關鎖間</rb><rt>kwæn-so-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>聖殿裏個</rb><rt>sing-din-li-go</rt><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>疑惑不決</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>監裏</rb><rt>kæn-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>侍衛</rb><rt>z-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>強</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>帶到</rb><rt>Ta-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>難道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>嚴禁</rb><rt>nyin-kying</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>行遍</rb><rt>ʽang-pin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>依從</rb><rt>I-dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>過如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>依從</rb><rt>*i-dzong*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>掛</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>殺掉個</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>舉</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>Kyüing-wông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>*kyin-tsing*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>切齒痛恨</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽ-tʽong-ʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦瑪列</rb><rt>Kyüô-mô-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊重個</rb><rt>tseng-djong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>*tsʽa-nying*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>辦</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丟大</rb><rt>Tiu-da</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>誇</rb><rt>kwʽô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>餘黨</rb><rt>yü-tông</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>隨從</rb><rt>ze-dzong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>化爲烏有</rb><rt>hwô-we-u-yiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>挨戶冊</rb><rt>a-wu-tsʽah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>搖惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>隨從</rb><rt>ze-dzong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>散開了</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>離遠</rb><rt>li-yün</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敗掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt></ruby>；<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>出於</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>敗掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>對頭</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Keh-go* *Tiu-da*, *pi* *da-kæ* *eo* “*Yiæ-su* *kyüông-seng* *keh-nyin*,” *tsao* *sæn* *nyin* *cʽih-go*. 箇個<u>丟大</u>，比大概謳「<u>耶穌</u>降生箇年」早三年出個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Ze-dzong; *ʽôh* *wô*, tʽing-sing. 隨從。或話，聽信。（原註）</sup>

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>依從</rb><rt>i-dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>進來</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒霉</rb><rt>tao-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

---

### Sd. VI.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教友</rb><rt>*kyiao-yiu*</rt><rb>怨恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教友</rb><rt>*kyiao-yiu*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>逐日個</rb><rt>djoh-nyih-go</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到家</rb><rt>tao-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>丟棄</rb><rt>tiu-kyʽi</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>喫用</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>揀擇</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>教友</rb><rt>*kyiao-yiu*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>稱讚個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感動個</rb><rt>ken-dong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>專管</rb><rt>cün-kwun</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>揀擇</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>伯羅哥羅</rb><rt>Pah-lo-ko-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>尼迦挪</rb><rt>Nyi-kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>提門</rb><rt>Di-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>巴米拿</rb><rt>Pô-mi-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼哥拉</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>使徒個</rb><rt>s-du-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>*S-du*</rt><rb>禱告仔</rb><rt>tao-kao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>行開</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒個</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-DI-FÆN</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利百地拿</rb><rt>Li-pah-di-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞力山太</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚會堂個</rb><rt>*jü-we-dông-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>*Z-di-fæn*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>捏造</rb><rt>nyiah-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*Z-ka*</rt><rb>挑動</rb><rt>tʽiao-dong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>誣告</rb><rt>vu-kao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>立起來</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>親口</rb><rt>tsʽing-kʽeo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>毀掉</rb><rt>hwe-diao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>改掉</rb><rt>kæ-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>Kong-we-li</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>坐間個</rb><rt>zo-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. VII.

<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米所波大米</rb><rt>Mi-sô-po-da-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>榮華個</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>本國</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>親族</rb><rt>tsʽing-dzoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>指引</rb><rt>ts-ying</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>迦勒底</rb><rt>Kyüô-leh-ti</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>哈蘭</rb><rt>Ha-læn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>死後</rb><rt>si-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>遷</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>毫無</rb><rt>ʽao-vu</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>立腳之</rb><rt>lih-kyiah-ts</rt><rb>處</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>將來個</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>周割禮個</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li-go</rt><rb>約</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>*Üô-pah-lah-hen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>*sang*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>十二房</rb><rt>jih-nyi-vông</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>氣</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗過</rb><rt>feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>法老</u>個</rb><rt>Fah-lao-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>*Fah-lao*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>王宮裏</rb><rt>wông-kong-li</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>到處</rb><rt>tao-cʽü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>打算弗來</rb><rt>tang-sön-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>剩</rb><rt>*dzing*</rt><rb>穀</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第二伐</rb><rt>di-nyi-vah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>認攏</rb><rt>nying-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>約瑟</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法老</rb><rt>Fah-lao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>合族</rb><rt>ʽeh-dzoh</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>十二房</rb><rt>*jih-nyi-vông*</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>遷</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘劍</rb><rt>Jü-kyin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>敘劍</u>個</rb><rt>Jü-kyin-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哈抹</rb><rt>Ha-meh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>買個</rb><rt>ma-go</rt><rb>墳地</rb><rt>veng-di</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>*tso-dzing*</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>罰咒過了個</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-ko-liao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>多起來</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>直等到</rb><rt>dzih-teng-tao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>認得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。 <sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>詐計</rb><rt>tsô-kyi</rt><rb>謀害</rb><rt>meo-ʽæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>丟掉</rb><rt>tiu-diao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。
