<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>年紀</rb><rt>Nyin-kyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>歲</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>報仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>忖弗到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>相打</rb><rt>siang-tang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>*Mo-si*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>和攏</rb><rt>ʽo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相害</rb><rt>siang-ʽæ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>中央人</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>賬</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>昨日</rb><rt>zô-nyih</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？』 <sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米甸</rb><rt>Mi-din</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>樹裏</rb><rt>jü-li</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>現出來</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt><rb>看看相</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」 <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>發抖</rb><rt>fah-teo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>腳裏個</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看見過</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>嘆息</rb><rt>tʽæn-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』 

<sup>35</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>覅其個</rb><rt>fæ-gyi-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>中央人</rb><rt>cong-nyiang-nying</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>樹裏</rb><rt>jü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>現出個</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>紅</u>海</rb><rt>ʽOng-hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>*keh-pin*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西乃</rb><rt>Si-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蕩邊</rb><rt>*dông-pin*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>詔書</rb><rt>tsiao-shü</rt><rb>傳授</rb><rt>djün-ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>更</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>依順</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反轉</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>轉</rb><rt>cün</rt><rb>念頭</rb><rt>nyiæn-deo</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞倫</rb><rt>Üô-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>快活記</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>殺牲</rb><rt>sah-sang</rt><rb>福禮</rb><rt>foh-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩洛</rb><rt>Mo-lôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>菩薩個</rb><rt>*bu-sah-go*</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>臉番</rb><rt>Lin-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>箇粒</rb><rt>keh-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>特爲</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>遷</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>過去個</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>44</sup> 「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>對證個</rb><rt>te-tsing-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>看見過個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt><rb>樣式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約書亞</rb><rt>Iah-shü-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接着仔</rb><rt>tsih-djôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶進去</rb><rt>ta-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>恩寵</rb><rt>eng-cʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>個</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所羅門</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>*sing*-din</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>Jün-r</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高個</rb><rt>kao-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>49</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>踏腳凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安頓</rb><rt>en-teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>造個</rb><rt>zao-go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」 

<sup>51</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>犟頭倔腦</rb><rt>gyiang-deo-gyüih-nao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靈性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>嘸周割禮</rb><rt>m-tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>違逆</rb><rt>we-nyih</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽAh-li</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>預先</rb><rt>Yü-sin</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>*sin-cü-nying*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>*kwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>傳授個</rb><rt>djün-ziu-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>戳起來</rb><rt>cʽoh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>切齒</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽ</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>*Z-di-fæn*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>開東</rb><rt>kʽæ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>懷聲</rb><rt>wæ-sing</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斬齊</rb><rt>tsæn-zi</rt><rb>擁上去</rb><rt>üong-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>脫勒</rb><rt>tʽeh-leh</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>後生個</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>揞個</rb><rt>kʽang-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt></ruby>。」<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>名下</rb><rt>ming-ʽô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>話好</rb><rt>Wô-hao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>睏熟了</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. VIII.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>歡喜個</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>虔心個</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>成殮</rb><rt>dzing-lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>份份</rb><rt>veng-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男男女女</rb><rt>nen-nen-nyü-nyü</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 34. 耶（穌）後（頭）34（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>散開間</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-kæn</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省城</rb><rt>*sang*-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斬齊</rb><rt>tsæn-zi</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>附的</rb><rt>vu-tih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>㗇㗇嘶</rb><rt>yüô-yüô-si</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>風癱個</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn-go</rt><rb>拐腳個</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>誇</rb><rt>kwʽô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大大小小</rb><rt>do-do-siao-siao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽信</rb><rt>tʽing-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>長久了</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽信</rb><rt>tʽing-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男男女女</rb><rt>nen-nen-nyü-nyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> （<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing-Ling*</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>臨着過</rb><rt>ling-djôh-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。）<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>*s-du*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>仔</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>按個</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸份</rb><rt>m-veng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>端正</rb><rt>tön-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>懊悔</rb><rt>ao-hwe</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>苦膽</rb><rt>kwʽu-tæn</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>惡念</rb><rt>ôh-nyiæn</rt><rb>縛住的</rb><rt>bo-djü-tih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>順路</rb><rt>*jing-lu*</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>鄉村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦薩</rb><rt>Kyüô-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>*lu*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古實</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古實</rb><rt>Kwu-jih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女王</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>干大基</rb><rt>Ken-da-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>太監</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>倉庫</rb><rt>tsʽông-kwʽu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>車子裏</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以賽亞</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇本</rb><rt>*keh-peng*</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇乘</rb><rt>keh-dzing</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>*li-hyiang*</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>*cü-kwu*</rt><rb>聚隊</rb><rt>jü-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奔攏去</rb><rt>peng-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讀</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以賽亞</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üÔ-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>開導</rb><rt>kʽæ-dao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>*ming-bah*</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>箇篇</rb><rt>keh-pʽin</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就話</rb><rt>ziu-wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>牽去</rb><rt>kyʽin-kyʽi</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>剪</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>卑賤個</rb><rt>pe-zin-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>公道個</rb><rt>kong-dao-go</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>出脫了</rb><rt>cʽih-tʽeh-de</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>太監</rb><rt>Tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請問</rb><rt>Tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>太監</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>阻隔</rb><rt>tsu-kah</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好使得個</rb><rt>hao-s-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>太監</rb><rt>*Tʽa-kæn*</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>太監</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>*Fi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>走上來</rb><rt>tseo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>太監</rb><rt>tʽa-kæn</rt><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞鎖都</rb><rt>Üô-so-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走過去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依路</rb><rt>i-lu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

---

### Sd. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>SAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>氣呼呼</rb><rt>kyʽi-hwu-hwu</rt><rb>狠呵</rb><rt>heng-hô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>*ta*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>*Sao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇路</rb><rt>keh-lu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>男男女女</rb><rt>nen-nen-nyü-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Sd. 19. 9, <sup>23</sup> *fæn* keh-go dao-li, *teng* *dông-deo* *fæn* keh-lu, *peng-veng* *tso* *z*. Sd. 19. 9, <sup>23</sup> 翻「箇個道理」，等蕩頭翻「箇路」，本文做字。（原註）</sup>

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>逼難個</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>踢着</rb><rt>tʽih-djôh</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>難個</rb><rt>næn-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嚇勒</rb><rt>hah-leh</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道其</rb><rt>dao-gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啞口無言</rb><rt>ô-kʽeo-vu-yin</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𠡒勒</rb><rt>te-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>茶</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>直</u>街</rb><rt>Dzih-ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>打聽</rb><rt>tang-tʽing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大數</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進來</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>難處</rb><rt>næn-cʽü</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祭司頭個</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>揀選個</rb><rt>kæn-shün-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>揚開</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>苦頭</rb><rt>kwʽu-deo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>Hweh-jün</rt><rb>眼睛裏</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>魚鱗</rb><rt>ng-ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>飯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>喫好</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>鮮索了</rb><rt>sin-soh-de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大馬色</u>個</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>特意</rb><rt>deh-i</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豈</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>越發</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>駁倒</rb><rt>poh-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日數</rb><rt>nyih-su</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>計謀</rb><rt>kyi-meo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>籃裏</rb><rt>læn-li</rt><rb>吊落</rb><rt>tiao-lôh</rt><rb>城腳沿</rb><rt>dzing-kyiah-yin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 37. 耶（穌）後（頭）37（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>結交</rb><rt>kyih-kyiao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講過了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>*Sao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>進進出出</rb><rt>tsing-tsing-cʽih-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大數</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*KEH-GO*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒瑪利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>德氣</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>*sing-du*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂大</rb><rt>Li-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉間個</rb><rt>deng-kæn-go</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以尼雅</rb><rt>Yi-nyi-yüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>眠牀裏</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>風癱</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以尼雅</rb><rt>Yi-nyi-yüô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>痊癒</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>整好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眠牀</rb><rt>min-zông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂大</rb><rt>Li-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒冷</rb><rt>Sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 38. 耶（穌）後（頭）38（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大比大</rb><rt>Da-pi-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多加</rb><rt>To-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>廣行</rb><rt>kwông-ʽang</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>周濟</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>滰好</rb><rt>gyiang-hao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樓頂</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>呂大</rb><rt>Li-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup> </sup>」

<sup>\[1\] To-kyüô, *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* tsʽ-loh. <u>多加</u>個解說就是雌鹿。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, soh-soh ziu ko-læ, m-nao tæn-kôh. 或話，速速就過來，嘸得好耽擱。（原註）</sup>

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>Tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>樓頂</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多加</rb><rt>To-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>裏外</rb><rt>li-nga</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>大比大</rb><rt>Da-pi-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐起來</rb><rt>zo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>提</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>特買</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皮匠</rb><rt>bi-ziang</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。
