### Sd. X.

<u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>KÆ-SAH-LI-ÜÔ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇營個</rb><rt>keh-ying-go</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>廣行</rb><rt>kwông-ʽang</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽÔ-pun-nyih</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫一驚</rb><rt>kyʽih-ih-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事體</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>*Tʽin-s*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>記得間了</rb><rt>kyi-teh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皮匠</rb><rt>bi-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 41. 耶（穌）後（頭）41（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>傭人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>身邊</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>侍候東</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-tong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>虔心個</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt><rb>營兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>晝過</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>屋頂</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>倦去</rb><rt>gyün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>開開來</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>四隻</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>宕落</rb><rt>dang-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>四隻腳個</rb><rt>s-tsah-kyiah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>殺殺</rb><rt>sah-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使弗得個</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>喫過</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收上</rb><rt>siu-zông</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>疑惑不決</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>顯靈是</rb><rt>hyin-ling-z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西門</u>個</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>思忖</rb><rt>s-tsʽeng</rt><rb>顯靈個</rb><rt>hyin-ling-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落去</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差來個</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尋個</rb><rt>zing-go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>百總</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>個</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>稱讚個</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>進來</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約帕</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>親眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>自家人</rb><rt>zi-kô-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>知己</rb><rt>cü-kyi</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等候間</rb><rt>teng-ʽeo-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起來</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>邊</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚攏間</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作興個</rb><rt>tsôh-hying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>隨便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>推辭</rb><rt>tʽe-dz</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>點鐘</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>雪刮亮個</rb><rt>shih-kwah-liang-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>31</sup> 『<u><ruby><rb>哥尼流</rb><rt>Ko-nyi-liu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>聽聞了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>記得間了</rb><rt>kyi-teh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皮匠</rb><rt>bi-ziang</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*Keh-tsao*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>毫無</rb><rt>ʽao-vu</rt><rb>偏曲</rb><rt>pʽin-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>做人個</rb><rt>tso-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>和平個</rb><rt>ʽo-bing-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>萬國人個</rb><rt>væn-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。）<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳個</rb><rt>djün-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做出來個</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*Ng-lah*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>個</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>制服個</rb><rt>cü-voh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> （<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掛</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>樹上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喝個</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>罪個</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>饒赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-liao</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>47</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*keh-tsao*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XI.

<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>LÆ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倦去</rb><rt>gyün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>*bo-leh*</rt><rb>四隻</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>宕落來</rb><rt>dang-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>四隻腳個</rb><rt>s-tsah-kyiah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>蟲豸</rb><rt>djong-dzi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鳥</rb><rt>tiao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>殺殺</rb><rt>sah-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使弗得個</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>馱進</rb><rt>do-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嘴巴裏</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>收上</rb><rt>siu-zông</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來間個</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差來</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>疑惑</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>六個</rb><rt>loh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約帕</rb><rt>Iah-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得救</rb><rt>teh-kyiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>*tsih-leh*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>*ling-djôh*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>記得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聖靈個</rb><rt>Sing-Ling-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩賜</rb><rt>eng-s</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>WE-LEH</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>逼難個</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>散開間個</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-kæn-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>親手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>風聲</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>Tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>堅固個</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感動個</rb><rt>ken-dong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 42. 耶（穌）後（頭）42（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大數</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着仔</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-ts</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>聚集攏</rb><rt>jü-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>徒</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起頭個</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 43. 耶（穌）後（頭）43（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞迦布</rb><rt>Üô-kyüô-pu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>革老丟</u>個</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立定</rb><rt>lih-ding</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>帶去</rb><rt>ta-kyʽi</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>庉間個</rb><rt>deng-kæn-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>馱去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 44. 耶（穌）後（頭）44（年）。（原註）</sup>

---

### Sd. XII.

<ruby><rb>當</rb><rt>TÔNG</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>動手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>。（<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>無酵節</rb><rt>Vu-kao-tsih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。）<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>抲來</rb><rt>Kʽô-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>十六個</rb><rt>jih-loh-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>逾越節</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>切心</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, feh-hyih-go. 或話，弗歇個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出個</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt><rb>頭夜</rb><rt>deo-yia</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>睏</rb><rt>kwʽeng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連住</rb><rt>lin-djü</rt><rb>兩根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢門口</rb><rt>lao-meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>守間</rb><rt>siu-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>透出</rb><rt>tʽeo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇間</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搭搭</rb><rt>tah-tah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>腰脅</rb><rt>iao-hyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>甦醒</rb><rt>su-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>Kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>手裏個</rb><rt>Siu-li-go</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>褪落</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>繳身帶</rb><rt>Kyiao-sing-ta</rt><rb>繫仔</rb><rt>kyi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞋帶</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt><rb>縛仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>外頭個</rb><rt>nga-deo-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>披仔</rb><rt>pʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇間</rb><rt>*keh-kæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>忖弗到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-tao</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真個</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>第二道</rb><rt>di-nyi-dao</rt><rb>守間</rb><rt>siu-kæn</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇道</rb><rt>keh-dao</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>鐵門</rb><rt>tʽih-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走出仔</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>街</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>醒頭到</rb><rt>sing-deo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希律</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-lih-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望個</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>馬利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>牆門</rb><rt>ziang-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>使女</rb><rt>s-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅大</rb><rt>Lo-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>聽聽看</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing-kʽen</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>聽出</rb><rt>Tʽing-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歡喜弗煞</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-feh-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>*lin*</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>*tu*</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔進去</rb><rt>peng-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>發憨了</rb><rt>fah-hen-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗歇個</rb><rt>feh-hyih-go</rt><rb>敲</rb><rt>kʽao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奇怪</rb><rt>gyi-kwa</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>覅</rb><rt>fæ</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, meng-meng-kʽen. 或話，問問看。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尋弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>審問</rb><rt>sing-meng</rt><rb>管守個</rb><rt>kwun-siu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>*sah-diao*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>耽擱間</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推羅</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西頓</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>斬齊</rb><rt>tsæn-zi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸心</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing</rt><rb>王個</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>近身官</rb><rt>gying-sing-kwun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯拉士都</rb><rt>Pah-lah-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求和</rb><rt>gyiu-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地方個</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>糧食</rb><rt>liang-zih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>純</rb><rt>jing</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>王個</rb><rt>wông-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>定規個</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>朝衣</rb><rt>dziao-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>王位</rb><rt>wông-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>*sing-ing*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>蟲</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>越發</rb><rt>*yüih-fah*</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>行開去了</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做好</rb><rt>tso-hao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tʽôh-fu gyi. 或話，託付其。（原註）</sup>

---

### Sd. XIII.

<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>LÆ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教師</rb><rt>kyiao-s</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼結</rb><rt>Nyi-kyih</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路求</rb><rt>Lu-gyiu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>養大個</rb><rt>iang-do-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>馬念</rb><rt>Mô-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>分派</rb><rt>feng-pʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>召</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 45. 耶（穌）後（頭）45（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Hyi-lih-go jü-hyüong. 或話，<u>希律</u>個乳兄。（原註）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西流基</rb><rt>Si-liu-kyi</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒拉米</rb><rt>Sah-lah-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>幫手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>穿過</rb><rt>Cʽün-ko</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>海島</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕弗</rb><rt>Pʽô-feh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>碰見</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴耶穌</rb><rt>Pô-yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士求</rb><rt>Z-gyiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>湊隊</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以呂馬</rb><rt>Yi-li-mô</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>翻出來</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>」）<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>作對</rb><rt>tsôh-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>誘惑</rb><rt>yiu-ʽôh</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>）<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>*ngæn-tsing*</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>奸詐</rb><rt>kæn-tsô</rt><rb>惡毒</rb><rt>ôh-doh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>魔鬼個</rb><rt>mo-kwe-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>弄掉</rb><rt>long-diao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>*ngæn-tsing*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>霧露</rb><rt>vu-lu</rt><rb>罩上</rb><rt>tsao-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>黑暗了</rb><rt>heh-en-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>摸來摸去</rb><rt>moh-læ-moh-kyʽi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>擋</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>PAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕弗</rb><rt>Pʽô-feh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>旁非利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別加</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別加</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走過去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>彼西底</u>個</rb><rt>Pe-si-ti-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知個</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>*shü*</rt><rb>讀好仔</rb><rt>doh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>勸化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>出門</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>興旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>迦南</rb><rt>Kyüô-nen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>七族個</rb><rt>tsʽih-dzoh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>判斷官</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-kwun</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四百</rb><rt>s-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒母耳</rb><rt>Sah-meo-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇派個</rb><rt>keh-pʽa-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>基士</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-z-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>*wông-ti*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>廢掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶西</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-si-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>合着</rb><rt>ʽeh-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, *yiu-teh* *doh*, kwʽun-shü. 本文字眼，有得讀，寬恕。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> 「<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>應許個</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>出道</rb><rt>cʽih-dao</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>傳開</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>回心轉意個</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>猜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>後如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>腳裏個</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>鞋帶</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亞伯拉罕</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>救個</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>應着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>Se-tsih</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗出</rb><rt>feh-cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>樹上</rb><rt>jü-zông</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>墳墓裏</rb><rt>veng-mo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>*yü-sin*</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>舉上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>*ing-hyü*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>第二首</rb><rt>di-nyi-siu</rt><rb>聖詩</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>*si*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>爛糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>*ing-hyü*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一首</rb><rt>ih-siu</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>爛糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爛糊了</rb><rt>læn-wu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>爛糊</rb><rt>læn-wu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, tseng-kyʽi z-ken, *z* *tsôh* eng-we *ka-shih*. 本文字眼，尊起事幹，是作「恩惠」解說。（原註）</sup>
