<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起來</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>赦罪個</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>*neng-keo*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>脫出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>罪愆</rb><rt>*ze-kyʽin*</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：<sup>41</sup> 『<ruby><rb>輕慢個</rb><rt>Kyʽing-mæn-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雖即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人話</rb><rt>nying-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>下伐</rb><rt>ʽô-vah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>*tsæ*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>聚會堂裏個</rb><rt>Jü-we-dông-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>散開</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>入</rb><rt>jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, keh loh nyih li-hyiang. 有得翻，箇六日裏向。（原註）</sup>

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>第二伐</rb><rt>Di-nyi-vah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>咸城裏</rb><rt>ʽen-zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>*fi-dæn*</rt><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>原</rb><rt>*nyün*</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>盡頭</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>別國人</rb><rt>Bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>傳遍</rb><rt>djün-pin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>攛掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>貴重個</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>鄉紳</rb><rt>hyiang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>撣掉</rb><rt>tæn-diao</rt><rb>腳裏個</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XIV.

<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>LÆ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>講勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>激動</rb><rt>kyih-dong</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攛掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>恩惠個</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>分開了</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 46. 耶（穌）後（頭）46（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>擁上來</rb><rt>üong-zông-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>得知了</rb><rt>teh-cü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>呂高尼</u>個</rb><rt>Li-kao-nyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>特庇</rb><rt>Deh-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>團圈個</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>LU-Z-TEH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>軟</rb><rt>nyün</rt><rb>腳骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>坐間</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>孃胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拐腳個</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>筆直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳起來</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂高尼</rb><rt>Li-kao-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>現化</rb><rt>yin-hwô</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>丟士</rb><rt>Tiu-z</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>*tsʽing-hwu*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希耳米</rb><rt>Hyi-r-mi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>領頭</rb><rt>ling-deo</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>城外</rb><rt>Dzing-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丟士</rb><rt>Tiu-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>廟裏個</rb><rt>*miao-li-go*</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>牽勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>編出個</rb><rt>pin-cʽih-go</rt><rb>花</rb><rt>hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>祭祭</rb><rt>tsi-tsi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扯開</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>性情</rb><rt>sing-dzing</rt><rb>相同個</rb><rt>siang-dong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虛花</rb><rt>hyü-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>以前個</rb><rt>Yi-zin-go</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>時常</rb><rt>*z-djông*</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>賜落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大熟個</rb><rt>da-joh-go</rt><rb>年成</rb><rt>nyin-dzing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>口糧</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滿心</rb><rt>mun-sing</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>Kông</rt><rb>箇番</rb><rt>keh-fæn</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>阻</rb><rt>tsu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗落</rb><rt>feh-lôh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>祭</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>攛掇</rb><rt>tsʽön-teh</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>圍</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>東</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特庇</rb><rt>Deh-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>入教</rb><rt>jih-kyiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒走轉</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>以哥念</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>門徒個</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神國裏</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>託付</rb><rt>tʽôh-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>穿過</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼西底</rb><rt>Pe-si-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>旁非利亞</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別加</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞大利</rb><rt>Üô-da-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做成個</rb><rt>tso-dzing-go</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>於是</rb><rt>Ü-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XV.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt><rb>辯駁</rb><rt>bin-poh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>教會</rb><rt>Kyiao-we</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒瑪利亞</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>*pih*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>Dô-kô</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>*cong-nying-go*</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>*s-peh*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>分別</rb><rt>feng-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>揹弗動個</rb><rt>pe-feh-dong-go</rt><rb>軛</rb><rt>ah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>門徒個</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>*tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介個</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>響</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神蹟</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西門</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>纔方</rb><rt>dzæ-fông</rt><rb>講過</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>咋樣</rb><rt>dza-nyiang</rt><rb>看顧</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>挑出</rb><rt>tʽiao-cʽih</rt><rb>一般</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>相對個</rb><rt>siang-te-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>16</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起好</rb><rt>kyʽi-hao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大闢</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>跌倒個</rb><rt>tih-tao-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拆掉間個</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao-kæn-go</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>樹起來</rb><rt>jü-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>剩落東個</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-tong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>吵擾</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>泥塑木雕個</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>*tsi-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>撮<sup> </sup>煞個</rb><rt>tsʽeh-sah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>*sang-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>從古以來</rb><rt>dzong-kwu-yi-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>*shü*</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傳開</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 勒；繩子等套住後用力拉緊</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>統</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>決意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聚隊</rb><rt>jü-de</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>稱呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴撒巴</rb><rt>Pô-sah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>推尊個</rb><rt>tʽe-tseng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一封</rb><rt>ih-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>帶去</rb><rt>ta-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>*sia*</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>別國個</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請請</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走出來</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吵擾</rb><rt>tsʽao-ziao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搖惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。）<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>聚攏東</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>決意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>親愛個</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>26</sup> （<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>捨出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>口傳</rb><rt>kʽeo-djün</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>決意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>擔頭</rb><rt>tæn-deo</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>幾樣</rb><rt>kyi-yiang</rt><rb>要緊個</rb><rt>iao-kying-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>泥塑木雕個</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>撮煞個</rb><rt>tsʽeh-sah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>*sang-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>康健</rb><rt>kʽông-gyin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>讀好仔</rb><rt>doh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>安慰個</rb><rt>en-w̆e-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>庉上</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>安安耽耽</rb><rt>en-en-tæn-tæn</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>定見</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>別人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>*hyüong-di*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>傳開</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, kyʽün-hwô-go. 或話，勸化個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>轉去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>間了</rb><rt>kæn-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>（<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>旁非利亞</rb><rt>Bông-fi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>怏起來</rb><rt>ông-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>巴拿巴</rb><rt>Pô-nô-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬可</rb><rt>Mô-kʽo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>揀着</rb><rt>kæn-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。
