### Sd. XVI.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特庇</rb><rt>Deh-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>路士得</rb><rt>Lu-z-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以哥念</u>個</rb><rt>Yi-ko-nyiæn-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定好個</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>*yü-kô*</rt><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>人數</rb><rt>nying-su</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>加湊</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>YI-KYING</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>弗呂家</rb><rt>Feh-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加拉太</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>每西亞</rb><rt>Me-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇推尼</rb><rt>Pi-tʽe-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>每西亞</rb><rt>Me-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特羅亞</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>夜頭</rb><rt>Yia-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立東</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>派來</rb><rt>pʽa-læ</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特羅亞</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>筆直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒馬特喇</rb><rt>Sah-mô-deh-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼亞波利</rb><rt>Nyi-üô-po-li</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓立比</rb><rt>Fi-lih-pi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一邊</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>頭一個</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>*Lo-mô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>新建個</rb><rt>sing-kyin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Sia* ‘*S-du* *ying* *djün*’ *go* *Lu-kyia* *keh* *z-ʽeo* *teng* *Pao-lo* *dong-de-go*; *sô-yi* *wô*, *ah-lah*. 寫〈使徒行傳〉個<u>路加</u>箇時候等<u>保羅</u>同隊個，所以話「阿拉」。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, di-ih pʽong-djôh-go. 或話，第一碰着個。（原註）</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>邊沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聚攏間個</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂底亞</rb><rt>Li-ti-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推雅推喇</rb><rt>Tʽe-yüô-tʽe-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>紫花</rb><rt>ts-hwô</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>*su-djông*</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>開導</rb><rt>kʽæ-dao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求懇</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>既經</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫頭</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>邪鬼</rb><rt>zia-kyü</rt><rb>附的個</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起課</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>得救個</rb><rt>teh-kyiu-go</rt><rb>路頭</rb><rt>lu-deo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>特買</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>難熬</rb><rt>næn-ngao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扭轉</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*Kyü*</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>賺頭個</rb><rt>dzæn-deo-go</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>失掉了</rb><rt>shih-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再話</rb><rt>*tsæ-wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>擁上來</rb><rt>üong-zông-læ</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>板子</rb><rt>pæn-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>打了</rb><rt>Tang-leh</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>記數</rb><rt>kyi-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>s-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>牢靠</rb><rt>lao-kʽao</rt><rb>管守</rb><rt>kwun-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢底</rb><rt>lao-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>牢牢靠靠</rb><rt>lao-lao-kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>上勒</rb><rt>zông-leh</rt><rb>大籠</rb><rt>do-long</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, fah-dông-zông. 或話，法堂上。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 腳枷</sup>

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>半夜過</rb><rt>Pun-yia-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>唱</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽東</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>Hweh-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>牢監裏個</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li-go</rt><rb>地基</rb><rt>di-kyi</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>登時</rb><rt>teng-z</rt><rb>開了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>鐐銬</rb><rt>liao-kʽao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>褪落了</rb><rt>tʽeng-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>S-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>調覺轉來</rb><rt>diao-kao-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>牢監個</rb><rt>lao-kæn-go</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>開了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>*S-nyüoh-s*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>西拉</u>個</rb><rt>Si-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋裏個</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>當時</rb><rt>Tông-z</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>*s-nyüoh-s*</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>傷痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>擺出</rb><rt>pa-cʽih</rt><rb>下飯</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歡歡喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>衙役</rb><rt>ngô-yüoh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>*s-nyüoh-s*</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>*u-sen*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>司獄司</rb><rt>S-nyüoh-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>：「<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>Kwun-fu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安安心心</rb><rt>en-en-sing-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>定罪過</rb><rt>ding-ze-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢監裏</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>斷斷</rb><rt>tön-tön</rt><rb>使弗得個</rb><rt>s-feh-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>衙役</rb><rt>Ngô-yüoh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>回報</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>*Kwun-fu*</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喫𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>陪</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>呂底亞</u>個</rb><rt>Li-ti-üô-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>見了</rb><rt>kyin-leh</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XVII.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>經過</rb><rt>kying-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>暗妃波里</rb><rt>En-fi-po-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞波羅尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒羅尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>老例</rb><rt>lao-li</rt><rb>走進去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一連</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>靠實</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>難</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>勸醒了</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>親近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>虔心個</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>貴重個</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>妒忌心</rb><rt>tu-gyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>無賴</rb><rt>vu-la</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搖攏</rb><rt>yiao-long</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孫</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>碰弗着</rb><rt>Pʽong-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖勒</rb><rt>tʽo-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孫</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>反亂</rb><rt>fæn-lön</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶孫</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>*Kwun-fu*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶孫</rb><rt>Yiæ-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>取保</rb><rt>cʽü-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>當夜</rb><rt>tông-yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇哩亞</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好如</rb><rt>hao-jü</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒羅尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一心</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>情願</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>kʽao-kyiu</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>內中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>貴重個</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖撒羅尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇哩亞</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>海口</rb><rt>hæ-kʽeo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>陪</rb><rt>Be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歸了</rb><rt>kyü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>PAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>滿</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>泥塑木雕</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>着急了</rb><rt>dziah-kyih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>碰着個</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 54. 耶（穌）後（頭）54（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ʽeh-zing-li tu cün-meng kong-vong nyi-su-moh-tiao. 或話，合城裏都專門供奉泥塑木雕。（原註）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以彼古羅</rb><rt>Yi-pe-kwu-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>士多亞</u>個</rb><rt>Z-to-üô-go</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>多講多話</rb><rt>to-kông-to-wô</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>惡像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>外國</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>再活個</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞略巴古</rb><rt>Üô-liah-pô-kwu</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>新鮮</rb><rt>sing-sin</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾樣</rb><rt>kyi-yiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> （<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>出門人</rb><rt>cʽih-meng-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>別樣</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>聽聽</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing</rt><rb>新鮮</rb><rt>sing-sin</rt><rb>行情</rb><rt>ʽông-dzing</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ʽô-tsôh pʽe-ts. 或話，下作坯子。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z*, Üô-liah sæn. 就是，<u>亞略</u>山。（原註）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞略巴古</rb><rt>Üô-liah-pô-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>纔始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>拜個</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>未</rb><rt>VI</rt><rb>識之</rb><rt>SIH-TS</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>JING</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>供奉</rb><rt>kong-vong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>宇宙</rb><rt>yü-dziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天地個</rb><rt>tʽin-di-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>起個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>廟宇裏</rb><rt>miao-yü-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡像</rb><rt>*ôh-ziang*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缺少長短</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao-dziang-tön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生氣</rb><rt>seng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>根脈</rb><rt>keng-mah</rt><rb>造出</rb><rt>zao-cʽih</rt><rb>萬國個</rb><rt>væn-koh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>限定個</rb><rt>ʽæn-ding-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>址界</rb><rt>ts-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>鑄就好</rb><rt>cü-dziu-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>尋尋</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>摸來摸去</rb><rt>moh-læ-moh-kyʽi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>然而</rb><rt>jün-r</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>*jih-dzæ*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>離</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遠</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>行動</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>詩</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>生個</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>當該</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>體質</rb><rt>tʽi-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手段</rb><rt>siu-dön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>靈巧</rb><rt>ling-kyʽiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>雕刻個</rb><rt>tiao-kʽeh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>*Yi-zin*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白個</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt><rb>時世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>眼開眼閉</rb><rt>ngæn-kʽæ-ngæn-pi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>各到各處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>限</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>定規個</rb><rt>ding-kwe-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, pa bu-sah go z-ken. 或話，拜菩薩個事幹。（原註）</sup>

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*Keh-sing*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>內中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞略巴古</rb><rt>Üô-liah-pô-kwu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>丟尼修</rb><rt>Tiu-nyi-siu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬哩</rb><rt>Da-mô-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Yüô-tin</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞居拉</rb><rt>Üô-kyü-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本都</rb><rt>Peng-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>剛剛</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革老丟</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>*kying-dzing*</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做樣</rb><rt>tso-yiang</rt><rb>手藝</rb><rt>siu-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>業</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每逢</rb><rt>me-vong</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>Teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>着急</rb><rt>dziah-kyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>扳駁</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>㪗㪗</rb><rt>tʽeo-tʽeo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾自個</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>乾淨個</rb><rt>ken-zing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶士都</rb><rt>Yiu-z-tu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>鐵隔壁</rb><rt>tʽih-kah-pih</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>Kwun</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革哩士布</rb><rt>Keh-li-z-pu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>Yia-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>顯靈</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>零</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>六個</rb><rt>loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>迦流</rb><rt>Kyüô-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞該亞</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>斬齊</rb><rt>tsæn-zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>攻擊</rb><rt>kong-kyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拉</rb><rt>l̆a</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>法堂上</rb><rt>fah-dông-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦流</rb><rt>Kyüô-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奸刁個</rb><rt>kæn-tiao-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>門份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>*tʽing*</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>理取</rb><rt>li-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>法堂上</rb><rt>fah-dông-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>PAO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>好幾</rb><rt>hao-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞居拉</rb><rt>Üô-kyü-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>堅革哩</rb><rt>Kyin-keh-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>剪</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt><rb>許過了</rb><rt>hyü-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>快到個</rb><rt>kwʽa-tao-go</rt><rb>節肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>肯個</rb><rt>kʽeng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>轉來</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>*Yiæ-lu-sah-leng*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提阿</rb><rt>En-di-ah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>*Læ*</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加拉太</rb><rt>Kyüô-lah-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>弗呂家</rb><rt>Feh-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>堅固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>門徒個</rb><rt>meng-du-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞力山太</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>口才</rb><rt>kʽeo-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>通透</rb><rt>tʽong-tʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>學習</rb><rt>ʽôh-jih</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>熱心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞居拉</rb><rt>Üô-kyü-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>百基拉</rb><rt>Pah-kyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歸去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>解說</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>仔細</rb><rt>ts-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>Üô-po-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞該亞</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>駁倒</rb><rt>poh-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>聖書</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

### Sd. XIX.

<u><ruby><rb>亞波羅</rb><rt>ÜÔ-PO-LO</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上路</rb><rt>zông-lu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>賜落來</rb><rt>*s-lôh-læ*</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>聽聞過</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>約翰</u>個</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>回心轉意個</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>後如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>當時</rb><rt>*tông-z*</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>各處</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>土話</rb><rt>tʽu-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>未來</rb><rt>mi-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>放膽</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>議論</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>心腸</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt><rb>石硬</rb><rt>zah-ngang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>謳出</rb><rt>eo-cʽih</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>推喇奴</u>個</rb><rt>Tʽe-lah-nu-go</rt><rb>書房裏</rb><rt>shü-vông-li</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 57. 耶（穌）後（頭）57（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>格外個</rb><rt>kah-nga-go</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>甚至於</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手巾</rb><rt>siu-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>圍裙</rb><rt>yü-gyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>病人個</rb><rt>bing-nying-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>脫掉了</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>惡鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>走江湖</rb><rt>tseo-kông-wu</rt><rb>趕</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>爲</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>業</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>惡鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>附的個</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>傳個</rb><rt>djün-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>*tseo-cʽih-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士基華</rb><rt>Z-kyi-wô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>惡鬼</rb><rt>Ôh-kyü</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>認得個</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>惡鬼</rb><rt>Ôh-kyü</rt><rb>附的個</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳過去</rb><rt>tʽiao-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打敗</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出膊</rb><rt>cʽih-poh</rt><rb>受傷</rb><rt>ziu-sông</rt><rb>逃出</rb><rt>dao-cʽih</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 58. 耶（穌）後（頭）58（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>稱讚</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>相信個</rb><rt>Siang-sing-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>明當當</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>話出來</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>邪法</rb><rt>zia-fah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>馱攏</rb><rt>do-long</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>估估</rb><rt>kwu-kwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>價鈿</rb><rt>kô-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>足值</rb><rt>tsoh-dzih</rt><rb>五千兩</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin-*liang*</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>行開</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做好了</rb><rt>tso-hao-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>定見</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>亞該亞</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>幫手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以拉都</rb><rt>Yi-lah-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>暫庉</rb><rt>dzæn-deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 59. 耶（穌）後（頭）59（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>鬧熱</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt><rb>非凡</rb><rt>fi-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>銀師父</rb><rt>nying-s-vu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>底米丟</rb><rt>Ti-mi-tiu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞底米</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi-go</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>神堂</rb><rt>jing-dông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>夥計</rb><rt>ho-kyi</rt><rb>銅鈿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>賺了</rb><rt>dzæn-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>少</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>謳攏</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星介</rb><rt>keh-sing-ka</rt><rb>行當</rb><rt>ʽông-tông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>師父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>行當</rb><rt>ʽông-tông</rt><rb>得利</rb><rt>teh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>統</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>勸醒</rb><rt>kyʽün-sing</rt><rb>帶掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事業</rb><rt>z-nyih</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看輕</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>女</rb><rt>nyü</rt><rb>菩薩</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞底米</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi-go</rt><rb>廟宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>*kʽong-pʽô*</rt><rb>化爲烏有</rb><rt>hwô-we-u-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>統</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>遍天下</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>供奉</rb><rt>kong-vong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亞底米</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi-go</rt><rb>威嚴</rb><rt>w̆e-nyin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞底米</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大勒緊</rb><rt>do-leh-kying</rt></ruby>！」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>胡嚨響亮</rb><rt>wu-long-hyiang-liang</rt><rb>紛紛不一</rb><rt>feng-feng-peh-ih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聚攏東個</rb><rt>jü-long-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>亂竄去</rb><rt>lön-tsʽön-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>走攏來個</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>推出來</rb><rt>tʽe-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立帶起</rb><rt>lih-tæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亞力山大</rb><rt>Üô-lih-sæn-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>合口齊動</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>謳起來</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞底米</rb><rt>Üô-ti-mi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大勒緊</rb><rt>do-leh-kying</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>今末個</rb><rt>kyih-mih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>反亂</rb><rt>fæn-lön</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>鬧事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>講好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>發散</rb><rt>fah-sæn</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。
