</sup>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>勸勸</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*Keh-deo*</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>設</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>計謀</rb><rt>kyi-meo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>決意</rb><rt>kyüih-i</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>馬其頓</rb><rt>Mô-gyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走轉來</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>Keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>庇哩亞</u>個</rb><rt>Pi-li-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所巴德</rb><rt>Sô-pô-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>帖撒羅尼迦</u>個</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞里達古</rb><rt>Üô-li-dah-kwu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西公都</rb><rt>Si-kong-tu</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>特庇</u>個</rb><rt>Deh-pi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦猶</rb><rt>Kyüô-yiu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提摩太</rb><rt>Di-mo-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb><u>亞西亞</u>個</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推基古</rb><rt>Tʽe-kyi-kwu</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特羅非摩</rb><rt>Deh-lo-fi-mo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特羅亞</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>無酵節</rb><rt>Vu-kao-tsih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子個</rb><rt>nyih-ts-go</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓立比</rb><rt>Fi-lih-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特羅亞</rb><rt>Deh-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 60. 耶（穌）後（頭）60（年）。（原註）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>七日個</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>頭日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𤖼</rb><rt>pʽah</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>講勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>長篇</rb><rt>dziang-pʽin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>半夜過</rb><rt>pun-yia-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>聚攏間個</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn-go</rt><rb>樓頂</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>燈亮</rb><rt>teng-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶推古</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽe-kwu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>檻頭</rb><rt>kʽæn-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>瞌𥅻</rb><rt>kʽeh-cʽong</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞覺</rb><rt>sah-kao</rt><rb>睏熟</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>三層樓</rb><rt>sæn-zeng-leo</rt><rb>跌落來</rb><rt>tih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>擋起</rb><rt>tông-kyʽi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落來</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撲倒</rb><rt>pʽoh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走上去</rb><rt>tseo-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼勒</rb><rt>pʽah-leh</rt><rb>餅</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一餐</rb><rt>*ih-tsʽæn*</rt><rb>飯</rb><rt>*væn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>講勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>長久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>活東了</rb><rt>weh-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心裏</rb><rt>*sing-li*</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>安慰了</rb><rt>en-w̆e-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞朔</rb><rt>Üô-shoh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>定見</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>步行勒</rb><rt>bu-ʽang-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞朔</rb><rt>Üô-shoh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米推利尼</rb><rt>Mi-tʽe-li-nyi</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基阿</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-ah-go</rt><rb>對面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>駛進</rb><rt>s-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒摩</rb><rt>Sah-mo</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>泊</rb><rt>boh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多基臉</rb><rt>To-kyi-lin</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利都</rb><rt>Mi-li-tu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>定見</rb><rt>ding-kyin</rt><rb>駛過</rb><rt>s-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>耽擱</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>好使得個</rb><rt>hao-s-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>五旬節</rb><rt>wu-shing-tsih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*PAO-LO*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米利都</rb><rt>Mi-li-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>想請</rb><rt>siang-tsʽing</rt><rb>教會裏</rb><rt>kyiao-we-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>來了</rb><rt>Læ-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>頭一日</rb><rt>deo-ih-nyih</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>謙虛</rb><rt>kyʽin-hyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>謀害</rb><rt>meo-ʽæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>*ng-lah-go*</rt><rb>益處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>隱瞞</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教訓</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>歸服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>縛住了個</rb><rt>bo-djü-liao-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>刑具</rb><rt>ying-gyü</rt><rb>禍難</rb><rt>ʽo-næn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>dziah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>意裏</rb><rt>i-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>高高興興</rb><rt>kao-kao-hying-hying</rt><rb>走完</rb><rt>tseo-wun</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>路程</rb><rt>lu-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>職份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>恩惠個</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>*su-djông*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走來走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神國個</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>隱瞞</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>過</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>設立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>監督</rb><rt>kyin-toh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一隊</rb><rt>ih-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>買來個</rb><rt>ma-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>厲害個</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>弗捨得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>箇隊</rb><rt>keh-de</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>歪斜個</rb><rt>hwa-zia-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt><rb>含勒</rb><rt>ʽen-leh</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>32</sup> 「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>將</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>*tʽôh*</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>恩惠個</rb><rt>eng-we-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>成就</rb><rt>dzing-dziu</rt><rb>爾拉德氣</rb><rt>ng-lah-teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>產業</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>尊起了</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi-liao</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看相</rb><rt>kʽen-siang</rt><rb>誰個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇雙</rb><rt>keh-shông</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>用場</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>*kong-ing*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>擺出</rb><rt>pa-cʽih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>懦弱個</rb><rt>no-ziah-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>施</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>講好仔</rb><rt>Kông-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>哭起來</rb><rt>kʽoh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>頭頸</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗅嗅</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴</rb><rt>cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>憂悶</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。

### Sd. XXI.

<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>筆直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥士</rb><rt>Ko-z</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅底</rb><rt>Lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帕大喇</rb><rt>Pʽô-da-lah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>Pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去個</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>開出去</rb><rt>kʽæ-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>順手邊</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>駛過</rb><rt>s-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>敘利亞</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推羅</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拋</rb><rt>pʽao</rt><rb>錨</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>卸載</rb><rt>sia-zæ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尋着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>箇頭個</rb><rt>*keh-deo-go*</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 卸船，《字語彙解》作「卸⿰舟載」</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>Nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt><rb>兒囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城外</rb><rt>zing-nga</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海塗上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>別開</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推羅</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>駛</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多利買</rb><rt>To-li-mæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>船路</rb><rt>jün-lu</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同隊個</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>個</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在內個</rb><rt>dzæ-nen-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>童女</rb><rt>dong-nyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>好幾</rb><rt>hao-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞迦布</rb><rt>Üô-kyüô-pu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>保羅</u>個</rb><rt>Pao-lo-go</rt><rb>繳身帶</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇根</rb><rt>Keh-keng</rt><rb>帶個</rb><rt>ta-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>別國人個</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>傷</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情願個</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>勸</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇作</rb><rt>hyih-tsôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>備好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt><rb>鋪蓋</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>行李</rb><rt>ʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>該撒利亞</u>個</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô-go</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>門徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>居比路</rb><rt>Kyü-pi-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拿孫</rb><rt>Nô-seng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋裏</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>庉處</rb><rt>deng-cʽü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>AH-LAH</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>高高興興</rb><rt>kao-kao-hying-hying</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>進去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>一打一</rb><rt>ih-tang-ih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>萬數</rb><rt>væn-su</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>熱心個</rb><rt>nyih-sing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>長通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割禮</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>*lao*</rt><rb>規矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚攏來</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>總</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到東了</rb><rt>tao-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>願心</rb><rt>nyün-sing</rt><rb>許間</rb><rt>hyü-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>馱出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>費用</rb><rt>fi-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>剃</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>虛個</rb><rt>hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>相信個</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>箇星介</rb><rt>keh-sing-ka</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>泥塑木雕個</rb><rt>nyi-su-moh-tiao-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>撮煞個</rb><rt>tsʽeh-sah-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>*sang-kʽeo*</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 原書此處錯印爲 kong-tao，據文意及 1898 年版改正</sup>

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>潔淨仔</rb><rt>kyih-zing-ts</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>潔淨個</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>來間</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>亂竄去</rb><rt>lön-tsʽön-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>響響</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>領勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> （<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特羅非摩</rb><rt>Deh-lo-fi-mo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去過了</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko-de</rt></ruby>。）<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>滿</rb><rt>Mun</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>搖動</rb><rt>yiao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>奔攏來</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拖出</rb><rt>tʽo-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>外頭</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>關了</rb><rt>kwæn-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>行情</rb><rt>ʽông-dzing</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>營裏個</rb><rt>ying-li-go</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽEn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔落</rb><rt>peng-lôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>營兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇手</rb><rt>hyih-siu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>走攏來</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兩根</rb><rt>liang-keng</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>鎖住</rb><rt>so-djü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做過</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>紛紛不一</rb><rt>feng-feng-peh-ih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>*tsʽin-tsong*</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗實</rb><rt>feh-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>營裏</rb><rt>ying-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>格步階</rb><rt>kah-bu-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>擁擠</rb><rt>üong-tsi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>營兵</rb><rt>ying-ping</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>懷懷響</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>帶進</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>營裏</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>希臘</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>作亂</rb><rt>tsôh-lön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>率領</rb><rt>seh-ling</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>強盜</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基利家</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大數</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>城裏個</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>兩句</rb><rt>*liang-kyü*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Keh Yiæ-gyih nying z Y. ʽE. 55 keh nyin tsôh-lön-go. 箇<u>埃及</u>人是耶（穌）後（頭）55（年）箇年作亂個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>應許了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>格步階裏</rb><rt>kah-bu-kæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>抈抈</rb><rt>yüih-yüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*Gyi-lah*</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>靜落了</rb><rt>zing-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希伯來</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

---

### Sd. XXII.

「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>叔伯</rb><rt>shoh-pah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>分剖個</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> （<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希伯來</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>土音</rb><rt>tʽu-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>靜辦</rb><rt>zing-bæn</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>*Pao-lo*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基利家</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大數</rb><rt>Da-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>養大</rb><rt>iang-do</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>迦瑪列</u>個</rb><rt>Kyüô-mô-lih-go</rt><rb>門下</rb><rt>meng-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>學習</rb><rt>ʽôh-jih</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>熱心</rb><rt>nyih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆位</rb><rt>cong-we</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>約來</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>晝過</rb><rt>tsiu-ko</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>掃羅</rb><rt>Sao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>定規</rb><rt>ding-kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道其</rb><rt>dao-gyi</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>亮光個</rb><rt>liang-kwông-go</rt><rb>照耀</rb><rt>tsiao-yiao</rt><rb>看弗見</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𠡒勒</rb><rt>te-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大馬色</rb><rt>Da-mô-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup>
