<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗禮</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滰掉</rb><rt>gyiang-diao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>走轉</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倦去</rb><rt>gyün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受個</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>聚會堂裏</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士提反</rb><rt>Z-di-fæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立間</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>顧着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>別國</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*Cong-nying*</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>懷起</rb><rt>wæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>Keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>胡嚨響亮</rb><rt>wu-long-hyiang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脫掉</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>灰塵</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>朝</rb><rt>dziao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>亂</rb><rt>lön</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>營裏</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>懷</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>緣故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>皮帶</rb><rt>bi-ta</rt><rb>綁</rb><rt>pông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>百總</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>走攏去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>買個</rb><rt>ma-go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>就是個</rb><rt>*ziu-z-go*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拷打</rb><rt>kʽao-tang</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走開</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>縛勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>確確實實</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh-jih-jih</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XXIII.

<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>樣樣</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>白粉</rb><rt>bah-feng</rt><rb>刷出個</rb><rt>shih-cʽih-go</rt><rb>牆</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>審</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>違背</rb><rt>we-be</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>Bông-pin</rt><rb>立東個</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𧮙</rb><rt>zôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嗎</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>壞</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>其中</rb><rt>*gyi-cong*</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>謳響</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>衆位</rb><rt>Cong-we</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>判斷了</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爭起來</rb><rt>tsang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>兩下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒土該</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>再活個</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>樣</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>胡嚨響亮</rb><rt>wu-long-hyiang-liang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>法利賽</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇邊</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>讀書人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>爬起來</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>爭論</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>尋弗出</rb><rt>zing-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>抵敵</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>既經</rb><rt>Kyi-kying</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>爭造</rb><rt>tsang-zao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>𤖼喫</rb><rt>pʽah-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>奪</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶進</rb><rt>ta-tsing</rt><rb>營裏</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>當夜</rb><rt>Tông-yia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>切身</rb><rt>tsʽih-sing</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>TAO-LEH</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>結了</rb><rt>kyih-leh</rt><rb>黨</rb><rt>tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>罰</rb><rt>Vah</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十多</rb><rt>s-jih-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>罰</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>甚大咒</rb><rt>zing-do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>煩</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將等</rb><rt>tsiang-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>準備</rb><rt>cing-be</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>姊妹個</rb><rt>tsi-me-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>營裏</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>犯人</rb><rt>Væn-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走過</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>烏蕩</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>約好</rb><rt>iah-hao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>察訪</rb><rt>tsʽah-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>聽信</rb><rt>tʽing-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>內中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十多</rb><rt>s-jih-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>準備</rb><rt>cing-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>單等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>應許</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>Tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>後生</rb><rt>ʽeo-sang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>千總</rb><rt>*Tsʽin-tsong*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>七十</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長槍手</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽiang-siu</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連夜</rb><rt>lin-yia</rt><rb>二更</rb><rt>nyi-kang</rt><rb>動身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>騎仔</rb><rt>gyi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>護送</rb><rt>wu-song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一封</rb><rt>ih-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>下文</rb><rt>ʽô-veng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>：

<sup>26</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>革老丟</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>呂西亞</rb><rt>Li-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>*sia*</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>請請</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>羅馬</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>帶勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>公會裏</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>不過</rb><rt>*peh-ko*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>關着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>計較</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>隨手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>送上來</rb><rt>song-zông-læ</rt><rb>見</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>康健</rb><rt>kʽông-gyin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>*gyi-go*</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>連夜</rb><rt>lin-yia</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提帕底</rb><rt>En-di-pʽô-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>馬兵</rb><rt>mô-ping</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>營裏</rb><rt>ying-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>該撒利亞</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇封</rb><rt>keh-fong</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>送上</rb><rt>song-zông</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>看好</rb><rt>kʽen-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>省</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基利家</rb><rt>Kyi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希律</u>個</rb><rt>Hyi-lih-go</rt><rb>王宮裏</rb><rt>wông-kong-li</rt><rb>押</rb><rt>ah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>間</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。

---

### Sd. XXIV.

<ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祭司頭</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞拿尼亞</rb><rt>Üô-nô-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>會講會話個</rb><rt>we-kông-we-wô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>帖土羅</rb><rt>Tʽiah-tʽu-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>總督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>謳來</rb><rt>eo-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>帖土羅</rb><rt>Tʽiah-tʽu-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>太太平平</rb><rt>tʽa-tʽa-bing-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>虧了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>先見之明</rb><rt>sin-kyin-ts-ming</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>光明正大</rb><rt>kwông-ming-tsing-da</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>國</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一處</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>*sô-yi*</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>時常</rb><rt>z-djông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>感恩個</rb><rt>ken-eng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>謝謝</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>煩</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>寬恕</rb><rt>kwʽun-shü</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>領徑</rb><rt>ling-kying</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>*kông*</rt><rb>兩句</rb><rt>*liang-kyü*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瘟人</rb><rt>w̆eng-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>教個</rb><rt>kyiao-go</rt><rb>頭腦</rb><rt>deo-nao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂西亞</rb><rt>Li-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走來</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>強</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>奪了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>原告</rb><rt>nyün-kao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*Næn-kæn*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>問</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>隨勒</rb><rt>ze-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>事幹</rb><rt>Z-ken</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>*jih-dzæ*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>總督</rb><rt>Tsong-toh</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>𩑦𩑦</rb><rt>w̆eh-w̆eh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>國個</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>判斷官</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-kwun</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講講</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>並</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>辯論</rb><rt>bin-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>吵亂</rb><rt>tsʽao-lön</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城裏</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>各到處</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>*kʽen-kyin*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>還弗出</rb><rt>wæn-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>*Tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>*zia*</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>祖宗個</rb><rt>tsu-tsong-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>想望個</rb><rt>siang-vông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>勉勵</rb><rt>min-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>對勒住個</rb><rt>te-leh-djü-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Fi-lih-z* *tso* *Yiu-tʽa-go* *tsong-toh*, *z* *Y*. *ʽE*. *53* *zông-dzing*. <u>腓力士</u>做<u>猶太</u>個總督，是耶（穌）後（頭）53（年）上任。（原註）</sup>

<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>*li-kʽæ*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>*Yiu-tʽa*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>歸來</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>周濟</rb><rt>tsiu-tsi</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>本國人</rb><rt>peng-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>獻</rb><rt>*hyin*</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>鬧熱</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>公會</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>錯處</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>話出來</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>謳響個</rb><rt>eo-hyiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再活個</rb><rt>tsæ-weh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>判斷了</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>除勒</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>*m-kao*</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>*soh-go*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>道理個</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>詳細</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>寬約</rb><rt>kwʽun-iah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>千總</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>呂西亞</rb><rt>Li-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落來</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>審結</rb><rt>sing-kyih</rt><rb>爾拉個</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>百總</rb><rt>pah-tsong</rt><rb>管押</rb><rt>kwun-ah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>寬待</rb><rt>kwʽun-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>供應</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>土西拉</rb><rt>Tʽu-si-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>猶太</u>個</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>講到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>節制個</rb><rt>tsih-cü-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>判斷個</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>便個</rb><rt>bin-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>屢次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>波求</rb><rt>Po-gyiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>非士都</rb><rt>Fi-z-tu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力士</u>個</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力士</rb><rt>Fi-lih-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>湊</rb><rt>tsʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>趣</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>給</rb><rt>coh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保羅</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>關東</rb><rt>kwæn-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 62. 耶（穌）後（頭）62（年）。（原註）</sup>
